--- entity_google_reviews [row 3 of 5] ---
review_id: 10287
entity_slug: gulf-shores-steamer
google_review_name: places/ChIJR1bZlp8HmogRL_gXPHvSRUM/reviews/Ci9DQUlRQUNvZENodHljRjlvT2xOa2FEUlpkWEJoZFZoa1JXSlRXamhaVjFSdk5sRRAB
rating: 5
review_text: We were really looking for good crab legs while down in gulf shores. This place was everything we were looking for. Everything from the location, service and the food was perfect.

We both decided to go with the crab boil that came with 2 crab leg groups, corn, potatoes, and gumbo for $40 at the time of writing this.

Our server was Paige I believe and she did fantastic! She took care of everything we needed. Was attentive without being over checking and we never wanted for anything.

All the crab came out of the shell in one piece, like it should. Gumbo was flavorful but not overly spicy. The amount of food was very much worth the value.

We don't come to the area but once a year but this has become a must stop when we are back in town.

--- entity_reviews [row 3 of 5] ---
id: 033bc31a-3361-4fe6-bab1-acae8d505780
entity_slug: gulf-shores-steamer
reviewer_name: Schwingschwa
rating: 5
body: We were really looking for good crab legs while down in gulf shores. This place was everything we were looking for. Everything from the location, service and the food was perfect.

We both decided to go with the crab boil that came with 2 crab leg groups, corn, potatoes, and gumbo for $40 at the time of writing this.

Our server was Paige I believe and she did fantastic! She took care of everything we needed. Was attentive without being over checking and we never wanted for anything.

All the crab came out of the shell in one piece, like it should. Gumbo was flavorful but not overly spicy. The amount of food was very much worth the value.

We don't come to the area but once a year but this has become a must stop when we are back in town.
